    <!-- Cloudflare Turnstile — explicit mode; auto-renders visible widgets, defers signin modal until open -->
    <script>
    function spotracTurnstileReady() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.cf-turnstile').forEach(function(el) {
            if (!el.closest('#signin-modal')) el._turnstileId = turnstile.render(el);
        });
    }
    document.addEventListener('show.bs.modal', function(e) {
        if (e.target.id !== 'signin-modal') return;
        var container = e.target.querySelector('.cf-turnstile, #turnstile-container');
        if (!container) return;
        if (container._turnstileId !== undefined) { turnstile.reset(container._turnstileId); return; }
        container._turnstileId = turnstile.render(container, { sitekey: '0x4AAAAAAC09P-b0vsh_w0Ib' });
    });
    </script>
    <script src='https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit&onload=spotracTurnstileReady' async defer></script>

    <!-- Grafana Faro (Frontend Observability / RUM) — replaces Datadog RUM,
         see docs/General/GrafanaMigration.md Phase 6.

         sessionTracking.samplingRate is the ONLY thing standing between us
         and blowing through Grafana Cloud's 50,000-session/month free tier
         in hours — estimated from ~50K sessions/~3hrs of real traffic,
         needs verifying against Grafana Cloud's usage page and tuning, not
         a precise number. Tune via the FARO_SAMPLE_RATE env var, no deploy
         needed to adjust. 0.20 (20%) would still exhaust the full monthly
         budget in ~15 hours at this traffic level — treat any rate above
         ~0.01 as a deliberate short-term validation burst, not steady-state.

         instrumentations/tracing structure confirmed against the actual
         @grafana/faro-web-sdk README (not just the bare wizard "prove
         connectivity" snippet, which omits instrumentations entirely and
         captures nothing): getWebInstrumentations() must be passed
         explicitly, and faro-web-tracing loads as a separate script after
         initializeFaro() has already run (it's a separate CDN package,
         added onto the live faro instance once loaded) — correlates
         frontend sessions with the backend PHP OTel traces (Phase 4).

         NOT ported from the old DD_RUM block: the excludedActivityUrls /
         beforeSend ad-network filtering (a real RUM slow-resource audit
         from 2026-07-08, see git history on this file) — Faro's equivalent
         API wasn't verified before shipping this, worth doing as a
         follow-up rather than guessing it blind. -->
            <script>
      (function () {
        var webSdkScript = document.createElement('script');
        webSdkScript.src = 'https://unpkg.com/@grafana/faro-web-sdk@2/dist/bundle/faro-web-sdk.iife.js';

        webSdkScript.onload = function () {
          window.GrafanaFaroWebSdk.initializeFaro({
            url: 'https://faro-collector-prod-us-east-2.grafana.net/collect/a2510e3129fe21cc775ff6d89baf58cd',
            app: {
              name: 'spotrac',
              version: '43379319',
              environment: 'prod',
            },
            instrumentations: window.GrafanaFaroWebSdk.getWebInstrumentations({ captureConsole: true }),
            sessionTracking: {
              samplingRate: 0.005,
            },
            ignoreErrors: [
              // Layout quirks — harmless, not real errors
              /^ResizeObserver loop limit exceeded$/,
              /^ResizeObserver loop completed with undelivered notifications$/,
              // Cross-origin scripts with no useful stack
              /^Script error\.$/,
              // Browser extension interference
              /chrome-extension:\/\//,
              /moz-extension:\/\//,
            ],
          });

          // Loaded after init — separate CDN package, added onto the live faro instance.
          var webTracingScript = document.createElement('script');
          webTracingScript.src = 'https://unpkg.com/@grafana/faro-web-tracing@2/dist/bundle/faro-web-tracing.iife.js';
          webTracingScript.onload = function () {
            window.GrafanaFaroWebSdk.faro.instrumentations.add(
              new window.GrafanaFaroWebTracing.TracingInstrumentation()
            );
          };
          document.head.appendChild(webTracingScript);
        };

        document.head.appendChild(webSdkScript);
      })();
    </script>

<script>        
var activeId = $(".navbar-mobile.navbar-level2 a.active").data("tab");
    
var $navTarget = activeId ? $('#'+activeId) : $();
var scrollTo = $navTarget.length ? $navTarget.position().left-20 : 0;     
    
$('.nav-wrapper').animate({scrollLeft: scrollTo}, 500);  
    

    $(document).ready(function(){
    if(window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches){
        // The viewport is less than 768 pixels wide
        //alert("This is a mobile device.");
        
        $(".navbar-level2 .dropdown-toggle").not(".spotrac-nav2-team-selector .dropdown-toggle").removeAttr("data-bs-toggle");
        $(".spotrac-nav2-team-selector > .dropdown-toggle").removeAttr("data-bs-toggle data-bs-auto-close").attr("href", "https://www.spotrac.com/nfl/teams");
    } else{
        // The viewport is at least 768 pixels wide
        //alert("This is a tablet or desktop.");
    }
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
    function closeMobileTeamMenus(exceptMenu){
        document.querySelectorAll('.spotrac-nav2-team-selector').forEach(function(selector){
            var toggle = selector.querySelector(':scope > .dropdown-toggle');
            var menu = selector.querySelector(':scope > .spotrac-nav2-team-menu');
            if(!menu || menu === exceptMenu) return;
            menu.classList.remove('show');
            selector.classList.remove('show');
            if(toggle){
                toggle.classList.remove('show');
                toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
            }
        });
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.spotrac-nav2-team-selector > .dropdown-toggle').forEach(function(toggle){
        toggle.addEventListener('click', function(event){
            if(!window.matchMedia("(max-width: 1023px)").matches) return;
            return;
            var selector = toggle.parentElement;
            var menu = selector ? selector.querySelector(':scope > .spotrac-nav2-team-menu') : null;
            if(!menu) return;
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            var isOpen = menu.classList.contains('show');
            closeMobileTeamMenus(menu);
            var shouldOpen = !isOpen;
            menu.classList.toggle('show', shouldOpen);
            selector.classList.toggle('show', shouldOpen);
            toggle.classList.toggle('show', shouldOpen);
            toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', shouldOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
            if(shouldOpen){
                var rect = toggle.getBoundingClientRect();
                document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--spotrac-nav2-team-menu-top', Math.round(rect.bottom) + 'px');
            }
        }, true);
        toggle.addEventListener('show.bs.dropdown', function(){
            var rect = toggle.getBoundingClientRect();
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--spotrac-nav2-team-menu-top', Math.round(rect.bottom) + 'px');
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function(event){
        if(!window.matchMedia("(max-width: 1023px)").matches) return;
        if(event.target.closest('.spotrac-nav2-team-selector')) return;
        closeMobileTeamMenus();
    });
});
</script>

<!-- Page specific script-->

<script type="module" src="https://media.spotrac.com/assets/app-Blo_s9as.js" defer></script>
<script>

// FILTER
	
$(document).on("change", "form#filter input", function(){

    $("#filtering").removeClass("d-none");
    
    var reference_id = $(this).data("reference");
        
    if($(this).is(":checked"))
    {
        $("input#"+reference_id+"_reference1").prop("checked",true).attr("checked",true);
        $("input#"+reference_id+"_reference2").prop("checked",true).attr("checked",true);
    }
    else
    {
        $("input#"+reference_id+"_reference1").prop("checked",false).attr("checked",false);
        $("input#"+reference_id+"_reference2").prop("checked",false).attr("checked",false);
    }
        
    //
	
    run_filter()

})
    
$(document).on("change", "form#filter select", function(){

    $("#filtering").removeClass("d-none");
    
    //
	
    run_filter()

})
    
$(document).on("click", "a.breadcrumb", function(){

    $("#filtering").removeClass("d-none");
    
    var reference_id = $(this).data("reference");
        
    $("input#"+reference_id+"_reference1").prop("checked",false).attr("checked",false);
    $("input#"+reference_id+"_reference2").prop("checked",false).attr("checked",false);
            
    if($(this).parents("li").find("a").length==1)
    {
        $(this).parents("li").remove();
    }

    $(this).remove();
    
    //
	
    run_filter()

})
    
// RESET FILTER
    
$(document).on("click", ".reset", function(e){
    
    e.preventDefault();
    
   
    $(".filtering-overlay").removeClass("d-none");

    $("table").addClass("blur")
    
    //
    
    document.location.href = 'https://www.spotrac.com/nfl/injured'
    
});

     
// SORT
    
$(document).on("click", "th.sort", function(){
   
    $("form#filter input[name='sort']").val( $(this).data('sort') );
    
    //
    
    run_filter()
    
});

$(document).on("click", ".injured2-timeline-sort", function(){

    var $button = $(this);
    var key = $button.data("sort-key");
    var direction = ($button.attr("data-sort-active") == "1" && $button.attr("data-sort-dir") == "desc") ? "asc" : "desc";
    var $table = $button.closest(".injured2-timeline-table");
    var $rows = $table.children(".injured2-timeline-data-row").get();

    $table.find(".injured2-timeline-sort")
        .removeClass("is-sorted-asc is-sorted-desc")
        .attr("data-sort-dir", "desc");

    $button
        .attr("data-sort-dir", direction)
        .attr("data-sort-active", "1")
        .addClass(direction == "asc" ? "is-sorted-asc" : "is-sorted-desc");

    $rows.sort(function(a, b){
        var aValue = parseFloat($(a).data(key)) || 0;
        var bValue = parseFloat($(b).data(key)) || 0;

        if(aValue == bValue) {
            return String($(a).data("player") || "").localeCompare(String($(b).data("player") || ""));
        }

        return (direction == "asc") ? (aValue - bValue) : (bValue - aValue);
    });

    $.each($rows, function(_, row){
        $table.append(row);
    });
});

$(document).on("click", ".injured2-player-display-toggle button", function(){

    var $button = $(this);
    var display = $button.data("display");
    var $wrapper = $button.closest(".injured2-tab-content");

    $button.closest(".injured2-player-display-toggle").find("button").removeClass("btn-dark").addClass("btn-outline-secondary");
    $button.removeClass("btn-outline-secondary").addClass("btn-dark");

    $wrapper.find(".injured2-player-display-panel").addClass("d-none");
    $wrapper.find(".injured2-player-display-panel[data-display-panel='" + display + "']").removeClass("d-none");

    init_table_scrolling();
    init_table_internal_sort();
});

	    
function run_filter()
{
    //$(".filtering-overlay").removeClass("d-none");
     
    $(".alert-filtering").removeClass("d-none");
   
    //
	
    var nextURL = $("form#filter").data('action');
    
    var selections = $("form#filter").serializeArray();
            
    var params = [];
    
    var year_exists = false;
    var team_direction = 'to';
    
    for(var i=0; i<selections.length; i++)
    {
        if(selections[i].name=='year')
        {
            year_exists = selections[i].value;    
        }
    }
    
    if(!year_exists)
    {
        params['year'] = [];
        params['year'].push( 2026 );
    }

    for(var i=0; i<selections.length; i++)
    {
        if(selections[i].name=='sort')
        {
            params['sort'] = [];
            params['sort'].push( selections[i].value );
        }
        else if(selections[i].name!='sort' && selections[i].value!='')
        {         
            if(!params[ selections[i].name ]) 
            {
                params[selections[i].name] = [];
            }

            params[selections[i].name].push( selections[i].value );
        }
    }
        
    var params_str = '';
    for (var key in params) 
    {
        var value = params[key];
        
        params_str += '/'+key+'/'+value.join("+");
    }
    
    
    nextURL = nextURL+'/_'+params_str;
    
   //$("table").addClass("blur")
        
    $.ajax({
        type: 'post',
        url: nextURL,
        data: 'ajax=table',
        success: function(data){
            $(".alert-filtering").addClass("d-none");

            $("#table-wrapper").html(data)
           
            init_table_scrolling();
            init_table_internal_sort();
            if(window.init_spotrac_tooltips) {
                window.setTimeout(function(){
                    window.init_spotrac_tooltips(document.getElementById("table-wrapper"));
                }, 0);
            }
        }
    }); 
    

	// update url history
    window.history.pushState({href: nextURL}, '', nextURL);
}
    
    
$("#csv-export").click(function(){

    $("table#table").table2csv('download', {filename: "nfl-injured-player.csv"});

})
    
// HIDE FILTER BASED ON VIEW
    
if ($(window).width() < 700)    
{
    $("#filter-desktop").remove();    
}
else if ($(window).width() >= 700)    
{
    $("#filter-mobile").remove();    
}
</script>
